## Rules applied (mechanical)

1. Read tools must be backed by the helper tier (`resolve` / `findBy*` / `search` / `getContext`).
   The registry currently carries 59 helper-tier tools, 57 read
   tools are still backed by a plain primitive — see "Helper-tier gaps" for the worklist.
2. `listAll` / `listPages` are never projected (unbounded reads).
3. Every list tool states its bound; every helper scan states its cap.
4. Mutating tools expose a `dry_run` input affordance.
5. `sensitive` / `requiresApproval` are stated on the tool.
6. No binary/download tool (resources photos, public_photos, uploads, exports, s3_exports) is projected.
7. `MCP_TOOL_OVERRIDES.json` is the only place a description may change; this script never re-derives one.
